server_tokens off;

client_max_body_size 1024m;
client_body_buffer_size 128k;

server_names_hash_bucket_size 128;
server_names_hash_max_size 10240;

ssl_dhparam /etc/nginx/ssl.crt/dhparams.pem;

ssl_session_cache   shared:SSL:10m;
ssl_session_timeout  5m;

proxy_read_timeout 1800s;

# https://mozilla.github.io/server-side-tls/ssl-config-generator/ intermediate configuration.
ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;
ssl_ciphers ECDHE-ECDSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:ECDHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256:ECDHE-ECDSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384:ECDHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384:ECDHE-ECDSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305:ECDHE-RSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305:DHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384:DHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256;
ssl_prefer_server_ciphers on;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/nginx-vhosts.conf:
    server {
        listen       44.214.133.234:80;
	listen		172.30.0.71:80;
        listen       127.0.0.1:80;
        listen       [::1]:80;

        server_name server.wbat.net 44.214.133.234;

        root   /var/www/html;

        # Pass all .php files onto a php-fpm/php-fcgi server.
        location ~ \.php$ {
                try_files $uri =404;

                fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
                include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
                fastcgi_index index.php;
                fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
                fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/webapps.sock;
        }

        include /etc/nginx/nginx-info.conf;
        include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
    }

    # HTTPS server
    #
    server {
        listen       44.214.133.234:443 ssl;
	listen		172.30.0.71:443 ssl;
        listen       127.0.0.1:443 ssl;
        listen       [::1]:443 ssl;
        http2 on;

        server_name  server.wbat.net 44.214.133.234;

        ssl_certificate         /etc/nginx/ssl.crt/server.crt.combined;
        ssl_certificate_key     /etc/nginx/ssl.key/server.key;

        root   /var/www/html;

        # Pass all .php files onto a php-fpm/php-fcgi server.
        location ~ \.php$ {
                try_files $uri =404;

                fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
                include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
                fastcgi_index index.php;
                fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
                fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/webapps.sock;
        }

        include /etc/nginx/nginx-info.conf;
        include /etc/nginx/webapps.ssl.conf;
    }

# configuration file /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params:

fastcgi_param  QUERY_STRING       $query_string;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_METHOD     $request_method;
fastcgi_param  CONTENT_TYPE       $content_type;
fastcgi_param  CONTENT_LENGTH     $content_length;

fastcgi_param  SCRIPT_NAME        $fastcgi_script_name;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_URI        $request_uri;
fastcgi_param  DOCUMENT_URI       $document_uri;
fastcgi_param  DOCUMENT_ROOT      $document_root;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_PROTOCOL    $server_protocol;
fastcgi_param  REQUEST_SCHEME     $scheme;
fastcgi_param  HTTPS              $https if_not_empty;

fastcgi_param  GATEWAY_INTERFACE  CGI/1.1;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_SOFTWARE    nginx/$nginx_version;

fastcgi_param  REMOTE_ADDR        $remote_addr;
fastcgi_param  REMOTE_PORT        $remote_port;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_ADDR        $server_addr;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_PORT        $server_port;
fastcgi_param  SERVER_NAME        $server_name;

# PHP only, required if PHP was built with --enable-force-cgi-redirect
fastcgi_param  REDIRECT_STATUS    200;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/nginx-info.conf:
location /nginx_status {
    # Enable nginx status page
    stub_status on;

    # Disable status page logging in access_log
    access_log off;

    # Allow access from 127.0.0.1
    allow 127.0.0.1;

    # Deny all the other connections
    deny all;
}

		deny all;

	location ^~ /.well-known/acme-challenge {
		root /var/www/html/;
		index index.php index.html index.htm;
		location ~ ^/.well-known/acme-challenge/(.+\.php)(/.+)?$ {
			include /etc/nginx/webapps_settings.conf;
		}
		location ~* ^/.well-known/acme-challenge/(.*(\.htaccess|\.htpasswd|\.user\.ini|\.env|\.git))$ {
			deny all;

			root /var/www/html/;

	}

# configuration file /etc/nginx/webapps_settings.conf:
try_files $uri $fastcgi_script_name =404;
root /var/www/html/;
fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/webapps.sock;
fastcgi_index index.php;
fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
set $path_info $fastcgi_path_info;
fastcgi_param PATH_INFO $path_info;
include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;

# configuration file /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf:
fastcgi_buffer_size 128k;
fastcgi_buffers 256 16k;
fastcgi_busy_buffers_size 256k;
fastcgi_temp_file_write_size 256k;
fastcgi_send_timeout 600;
fastcgi_read_timeout 600;
fastcgi_intercept_errors on;
fastcgi_param HTTP_PROXY "";

			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;

}

	root "/home/tellerstec/domains/learn.tellerstech.com/public_html";
	index index.php index.html index.htm;
	ssl_certificate /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/learn.tellerstech.com.cert.combined;
	ssl_certificate_key /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/learn.tellerstech.com.key;
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.ssl.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

server
{
# CloudFront origin gate — DirectAdmin parent CUSTOM for tellerstech.com
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Install (do not commit the real secret):
#   export CLOUDFRONT_ORIGIN_SECRET='…'   # same value as CF origin custom header
#   sudo ./scripts/install-cloudfront-origin-gate.sh
#
# Or copy to:
#   /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/tellerstech.com.cust_nginx
# replace the shared-secret placeholder in the if ($http_x_cloudfront_secret …) line, then:
#   echo "action=rewrite&value=nginx" >> /usr/local/directadmin/data/task.queue
#   /usr/local/directadmin/dataskq d
#   sudo /home/tellerstec/bin/fix-nginx-loopback-listeners.sh --verify
#
# Prefer: sudo …/tellerstech-landing/scripts/install-cloudfront-origin-gate.sh
# (CI deploy on main runs that installer idempotently.)
# Parent DOMAIN.cust_nginx is applied to ALL tellerstech.com server{} blocks.
# Enforce only for the CloudFront origin hostname.
#
set $cf_need_secret 0;
if ($host = "origin.tellerstech.com") {
    set $cf_need_secret 1;
}
if ($host = "www.origin.tellerstech.com") {
    set $cf_need_secret 1;
}
set $cf_deny "$cf_need_secret";
if ($http_x_cloudfront_secret = "dBzJlk6lrbfn2XWaufIQ6uGkKfxCRfWDvhcZ6jmN") {
    set $cf_deny 0;
}
if ($request_uri ~* "^/.well-known/acme-challenge/") {
    set $cf_deny 0;
}
if ($cf_deny = 1) {
    return 403;
}
# Block WordPress xmlrpc (brute-force / amplification). Applies to all
# tellerstech.com vhosts via parent CUSTOM — same as the origin gate above.
if ($request_uri ~* "^/xmlrpc\.php") {
    return 403;
}
	listen 44.214.133.234:80;
	listen 172.30.0.87:80;
	listen 127.0.0.1:80;
	listen [::1]:80;
	listen 172.30.0.71:80;
	server_name origin.tellerstech.com www.origin.tellerstech.com;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/tellerstech.com.origin.log;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/tellerstech.com.origin.bytes bytes;
	error_log /var/log/nginx/domains/tellerstech.com.origin.error.log;
	root "/home/tellerstec/domains/tellerstech.com/public_html";
	index index.php index.html index.htm;
	if ($http_x_forwarded_proto != 'https') {
		return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
	}
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

server
{
	listen 44.214.133.234:80;
	listen 172.30.0.87:80;
	listen 127.0.0.1:80;
	listen [::1]:80;
	listen 172.30.0.71:80;
	server_name codeduck.ai www.codeduck.ai;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/codeduck.ai.log;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/codeduck.ai.bytes bytes;
	error_log /var/log/nginx/domains/codeduck.ai.error.log;
	root "/home/tellerstec/domains/codeduck.ai/public_html";
	index index.php index.html index.htm;
	# Mail auto configuration (Thunderbird)
	location = "/.well-known/autoconfig/mail/config-v1.1.xml" {
		proxy_pass http://unix:/usr/local/directadmin/shared/internal.sock;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For  $remote_addr;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
	}
# Code Duck vanity redirects — DirectAdmin nginx CUSTOM3 for codeduck.ai
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Path-preserving 301s to canonical tellerstech.com Code Duck URLs with domain
# UTM attribution. DirectAdmin injects this into every codeduck.ai server{}
# block (apex + www, HTTP + HTTPS).
#
# Install: Admin Level → Custom HTTPD Configurations → codeduck.ai →
# nginx CUSTOM3 tab, or copy to:
#   /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/codeduck.ai.cust_nginx.3
# then:
#   echo "action=rewrite&value=nginx" >> /usr/local/directadmin/data/task.queue
#   /usr/local/directadmin/dataskq d
#   sudo /home/tellerstec/bin/fix-nginx-loopback-listeners.sh --verify
#
# codeduck.ai: same rules with utm_source=codeduck.ai.
#
# Server-context rewrites, NOT location{} blocks — DA's generated vhost already
# declares "location /", so a duplicate would break nginx -t. The trailing "?"
# in each replacement drops the inbound query string, so the domain UTMs are
# authoritative (no doubled utm_* from shared links).
# Let ACME http-01 challenges through for cert issuance/renewal.
rewrite ^(/\.well-known/.*)$ $1 break;
# Site-root passthrough for the shared trackers: sponsor /go/<slug> and SIW
# episode /siw/go/<key>/ resolve at the site root, never under the CD prefix.
rewrite ^(/(?:siw/)?go/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com$1?utm_source=codeduck.ai&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Homepage → Code Duck landing.
rewrite ^/$ https://www.tellerstech.com/labs/code-duck/?utm_source=codeduck.ai&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Everything else keeps its path under the Code Duck prefix (future subpages).
rewrite ^(/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com/labs/code-duck$1?utm_source=codeduck.ai&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}

try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;

server
{
	listen 44.214.133.234:80;
	listen 172.30.0.87:80;
	listen 127.0.0.1:80;
	listen [::1]:80;
	listen 172.30.0.71:80;
	server_name codeduck.io www.codeduck.io;

	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/codeduck.io.bytes bytes;
	error_log /var/log/nginx/domains/codeduck.io.error.log;
	root "/home/tellerstec/domains/codeduck.io/public_html";
	index index.php index.html index.htm;
	# Mail auto configuration (Thunderbird)
	location = "/.well-known/autoconfig/mail/config-v1.1.xml" {
		proxy_pass http://unix:/usr/local/directadmin/shared/internal.sock;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For  $remote_addr;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
	}
# Code Duck vanity redirects — DirectAdmin nginx CUSTOM3 for codeduck.io
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Path-preserving 301s to canonical tellerstech.com Code Duck URLs with domain
# UTM attribution. DirectAdmin injects this into every codeduck.io server{}
# block (apex + www, HTTP + HTTPS).
#
# Install: Admin Level → Custom HTTPD Configurations → codeduck.io →
# nginx CUSTOM3 tab, or copy to:
#   /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/codeduck.io.cust_nginx.3
# then:
#   echo "action=rewrite&value=nginx" >> /usr/local/directadmin/data/task.queue
#   /usr/local/directadmin/dataskq d
#   sudo /home/tellerstec/bin/fix-nginx-loopback-listeners.sh --verify
#
# codeduck.ai: same rules with utm_source=codeduck.ai.
#
# Server-context rewrites, NOT location{} blocks — DA's generated vhost already
# declares "location /", so a duplicate would break nginx -t. The trailing "?"
# in each replacement drops the inbound query string, so the domain UTMs are
# authoritative (no doubled utm_* from shared links).
# Let ACME http-01 challenges through for cert issuance/renewal.
rewrite ^(/\.well-known/.*)$ $1 break;
# Site-root passthrough for the shared trackers: sponsor /go/<slug> and SIW
# episode /siw/go/<key>/ resolve at the site root, never under the CD prefix.
rewrite ^(/(?:siw/)?go/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com$1?utm_source=codeduck.io&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Homepage → Code Duck landing.
rewrite ^/$ https://www.tellerstech.com/labs/code-duck/?utm_source=codeduck.io&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Everything else keeps its path under the Code Duck prefix (future subpages).
rewrite ^(/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com/labs/code-duck$1?utm_source=codeduck.io&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

# Ship It Weekly vanity redirects — DirectAdmin nginx CUSTOM3 for ship.tf
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Path-preserving 301s to canonical tellerstech.com podcast URLs with domain
# UTM attribution. DirectAdmin injects this into every ship.tf
# server{} block (apex + www, HTTP + HTTPS).
#
# Install: Admin Level → Custom HTTPD Configurations → ship.tf →
# nginx CUSTOM3 tab, or copy to:
#   /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/ship.tf.cust_nginx.3
# then:
#   echo "action=rewrite&value=nginx" >> /usr/local/directadmin/data/task.queue
#   /usr/local/directadmin/dataskq d
#   sudo /home/tellerstec/bin/fix-nginx-loopback-listeners.sh --verify
#
# shipitweekly.io / ship.tf: same rules with utm_source set to that host.
#
# Server-context rewrites, NOT location{} blocks — DA's generated vhost already
# declares "location /", so a duplicate would break nginx -t. The trailing "?"
# in each replacement drops the inbound query string, so the domain UTMs are
# authoritative (no doubled utm_* from shared links).
# Let ACME http-01 challenges through for cert issuance/renewal.
rewrite ^(/\.well-known/.*)$ $1 break;
# Episode short-link: /go/56 (or /siw/go/56) → site-root /siw/go/<key>/ which
# resolves by episode number / slug / slug-prefix (siw_go_handle_request in
# tellerstech-siw-episode.php). On THIS host /go/ means "episode go" — the
# sponsor /go/<slug> click tracker lives only on www.tellerstech.com.
rewrite ^/(?:siw/)?go/([^/]+)/?$ https://www.tellerstech.com/siw/go/$1/?utm_source=ship.tf&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Homepage → podcast landing.
rewrite ^/$ https://www.tellerstech.com/ship-it-weekly-podcast/?utm_source=ship.tf&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Everything else (episode slugs, /media-kit/, /host/, /guests/…, /page/N/)
# keeps its path under the podcast prefix.
rewrite ^(/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com/ship-it-weekly-podcast$1?utm_source=ship.tf&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

# Ship It Weekly vanity redirects — DirectAdmin nginx CUSTOM3 for shipitweekly.fm
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Path-preserving 301s to canonical tellerstech.com podcast URLs with domain
# UTM attribution. DirectAdmin injects this into every shipitweekly.fm
# server{} block (apex + www, HTTP + HTTPS).
#
# Install: Admin Level → Custom HTTPD Configurations → shipitweekly.fm →
# nginx CUSTOM3 tab, or copy to:
#   /usr/local/directadmin/data/users/tellerstec/domains/shipitweekly.fm.cust_nginx.3
# then:
#   echo "action=rewrite&value=nginx" >> /usr/local/directadmin/data/task.queue
#   /usr/local/directadmin/dataskq d
#   sudo /home/tellerstec/bin/fix-nginx-loopback-listeners.sh --verify
#
# shipitweekly.io / ship.tf: same rules with utm_source set to that host.
#
# Server-context rewrites, NOT location{} blocks — DA's generated vhost already
# declares "location /", so a duplicate would break nginx -t. The trailing "?"
# in each replacement drops the inbound query string, so the domain UTMs are
# authoritative (no doubled utm_* from shared links).
# Let ACME http-01 challenges through for cert issuance/renewal.
rewrite ^(/\.well-known/.*)$ $1 break;
# Episode short-link: /go/56 (or /siw/go/56) → site-root /siw/go/<key>/ which
# resolves by episode number / slug / slug-prefix (siw_go_handle_request in
# tellerstech-siw-episode.php). On THIS host /go/ means "episode go" — the
# sponsor /go/<slug> click tracker lives only on www.tellerstech.com.
rewrite ^/(?:siw/)?go/([^/]+)/?$ https://www.tellerstech.com/siw/go/$1/?utm_source=shipitweekly.fm&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Homepage → podcast landing.
rewrite ^/$ https://www.tellerstech.com/ship-it-weekly-podcast/?utm_source=shipitweekly.fm&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
# Everything else (episode slugs, /media-kit/, /host/, /guests/…, /page/N/)
# keeps its path under the podcast prefix.
rewrite ^(/.*)$ https://www.tellerstech.com/ship-it-weekly-podcast$1?utm_source=shipitweekly.fm&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=domain? permanent;
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/tellerstec.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}

{

	listen 172.30.0.87:80;
	server_name test.com www.test.com;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/test.com.log;
	access_log /var/log/nginx/domains/test.com.bytes bytes;
	error_log /var/log/nginx/domains/test.com.error.log;
	root "/home/test2/domains/test.com/public_html";
	index index.php index.html index.htm;
	# Mail auto configuration (Thunderbird)
	location = "/.well-known/autoconfig/mail/config-v1.1.xml" {
		proxy_pass http://unix:/usr/local/directadmin/shared/internal.sock;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For  $remote_addr;
		proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
	}
	include /etc/nginx/webapps.conf;
		# use fastcgi for all php files
		location ~ \.php$
		{
			try_files $uri =404;
			fastcgi_split_path_info ^(.+\.php)(/.+)$;
			include /etc/nginx/fastcgi_params;
			fastcgi_index index.php;
			fastcgi_param SCRIPT_FILENAME $document_root$fastcgi_script_name;
			if ( $skip_cache ~ "^$" ) {
				set $skip_cache 1;
			}
			fastcgi_cache_bypass $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_no_cache $skip_cache;
			fastcgi_cache FASTCGICACHE;
			fastcgi_cache_valid 60m;
			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)
			{
				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/test2.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
try_files $uri $uri/ /index.php?$args ;
}
}

			if (-f $request_filename)

			}

			include /etc/nginx/nginx_limits.conf;
			if (-f $request_filename)

				fastcgi_pass unix:/usr/local/php82/sockets/admin.sock;
			}
		}
location / {
